## DecoderOptions Class -- Complete Option Handling Code

### 1. Core Class: `DecoderOptions` (Binding Subclass)

**File:** `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\prop\binding\decoderoptions.h` (lines 44-63)
**File:** `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\prop\binding\decoderoptions.cpp` (lines 45-157)

#### Constructor -- Option Type Dispatch (lines 45-102)

The constructor iterates over `srd_decoder_option` entries from `dec->options` and creates the appropriate `Property` subclass based on the GVariant type of `opt->def`:

```cpp
DecoderOptions::DecoderOptions(pv::data::DecoderStack* decoder_stack, data::decode::Decoder *decoder) :
    Binding(),
    _decoder(decoder)
{
    // ...
    for (GSList *l = dec->options; l; l = l->next)
    { 
        const srd_decoder_option *const opt = (srd_decoder_option*)l->data;

        // Language/i18n handling for option description
        // ...

        const QString name = QString::fromUtf8(desc_str);

        const Property::Getter getter = bind(&DecoderOptions::getter, this, opt->id);
        const Property::Setter setter = bind(&DecoderOptions::setter, this, opt->id, _1);

        Property *prop = NULL;

        if (opt->values)                              // Has enum values list
            prop = bind_enum(name, opt, getter, setter);
        else if (g_variant_is_of_type(opt->def, G_VARIANT_TYPE("d")))   // double
            prop = new Double(name, name, 2, "", none, none, getter, setter);
        else if (g_variant_is_of_type(opt->def, G_VARIANT_TYPE("x")))   // int64
            prop = new Int(name, name, "", none, getter, setter);
        else if (g_variant_is_of_type(opt->def, G_VARIANT_TYPE("s")))   // string
            prop = new String(name, name, getter, setter);
        else
            continue;   // SKIP unsupported types

        _properties.push_back(prop);
    }
}
```

**Full list of GVariant types supported:**

| Priority | GVariant Type String | C Type | Property Class | Widget |
|----------|---------------------|--------|----------------|--------|
| 1st | (has `opt->values`) | any | `Enum` | `DsComboBox` |
| 2nd | `"d"` | `double` | `Double` | `DsDoubleSpinBox` |
| 3rd | `"x"` | `int64` | `Int` | `DsSpinBox` |
| 4th | `"s"` | `string` | `String` | `QLineEdit` |

**Important:** The `Int` property class internally supports 7 GVariant subtypes when reading/writing (see `int.cpp` lines 91-130):
- `G_VARIANT_TYPE_BYTE` (`"y"`) -- `g_variant_get_byte` / `g_variant_new_byte`
- `G_VARIANT_TYPE_INT16` (`"n"`) -- `g_variant_get_int16` / `g_variant_new_int16`
- `G_VARIANT_TYPE_UINT16` (`"q"`) -- `g_variant_get_uint16` / `g_variant_new_uint16`
- `G_VARIANT_TYPE_INT32` (`"i"`) -- `g_variant_get_int32` / `g_variant_new_int32`
- `G_VARIANT_TYPE_UINT32` (`"u"`) -- `g_variant_get_uint32` / `g_variant_new_int32` (note: bug on line 176, writes as int32)
- `G_VARIANT_TYPE_INT64` (`"x"`) -- `g_variant_get_int64` / `g_variant_new_int64`
- `G_VARIANT_TYPE_UINT64` (`"t"`) -- `g_variant_get_uint64` / `g_variant_new_uint64`

However, the `DecoderOptions` constructor only dispatches on `"x"` (int64) for the Int property. Other integer types (byte, int16, uint16, int32, uint32, uint64) would fall through to `continue` (skipped) unless they have an `opt->values` list (in which case they become `Enum`).

**Notably absent from DecoderOptions:** The `Bool` property class exists in the codebase (`pv/prop/bool.h`/`bool.cpp`) using `QCheckBox`, but `DecoderOptions` does **not** create `Bool` properties. Boolean options would only be handled if they appear in `opt->values` as an enum.

---

#### `bind_enum` -- Enum Option Creation (lines 104-116)

```cpp
Property* DecoderOptions::bind_enum(
    const QString &name, const srd_decoder_option *option,
    Property::Getter getter, Property::Setter setter)
{
    std::vector<std::pair<GVariant*, QString> > values;
    for (GSList *l = option->values; l; l = l->next) {
        GVariant *const var = (GVariant*)l->data;
        assert(var);
        values.push_back(make_pair(var, print_gvariant(var)));
    }
    return new Enum(name, name, values, getter, setter);
}
```

Each enum value is a `pair<GVariant*, QString>` where the QString is produced by `Binding::print_gvariant()` (binding.cpp lines 92-106), which handles `"s"` type specially (UTF-8 string) and uses `g_variant_print()` for everything else.

---

#### `getter` -- Reading Option Values (lines 118-150)

```cpp
GVariant* DecoderOptions::getter(const char *id)
{
    GVariant *val = NULL;
    // First: look in the decoder's already-set options map
    const map<string, GVariant*>& options = _decoder->options();
    auto iter = options.find(id);
    if (iter != options.end())
        val = (*iter).second;
    else {
        // Fallback: find the default value from srd_decoder_option->def
        for (GSList *l = _decoder->decoder()->options; l; l = l->next) {
            const srd_decoder_option *const opt = (srd_decoder_option*)l->data;
            if (strcmp(opt->id, id) == 0) {
                val = opt->def;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    if (val)
        g_variant_ref(val);  // Caller must unref
    return val;
}
```

---

#### `setter` -- Committing Option Values (lines 152-156)

```cpp
void DecoderOptions::setter(const char *id, GVariant *value)
{
    assert(_decoder);
    _decoder->set_option(id, value);
}
```

This delegates to `Decoder::set_option()` (`c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\data\decode\decoder.cpp`, lines 31-39):

```cpp
void Decoder::set_option(const char *id, GVariant *value)
{
    assert(value);
    if (_options[id]) {
        g_variant_unref(_options[id]);
    }
    g_variant_ref(value);
    _options[id] = value;
}
```

The options are stored in `Decoder::_options` which is `std::map<std::string, GVariant*>` (decoder.h line 117).

---

### 2. The `srd_decoder_option` Struct

**File:** `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\libsigrokdecode\libsigrokdecode.h` (lines 326-332)

```c
struct srd_decoder_option {
  char *id;       // Option identifier (used as key in getter/setter)
  char *idn;      // Language text source id (for i18n)
  char *desc;     // Human-readable description
  GVariant *def;  // Default value (also determines the GVariant type)
  GSList *values; // List of GVariant* for enum values; NULL if not enum
};
```

The `def` field serves dual purpose: it provides the default value AND its GVariant type determines which Property subclass is created. The `values` field, when non-NULL, causes the option to be treated as an enum regardless of the `def` type.

### 5. Full Flow: "Add Protocol" to Decoder Being Configured

**Step 1: User clicks "Add Protocol" in ProtocolDock**

`ProtocolDock::add_protocol_by_id()` at `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\dock\protocoldock.cpp` (lines 460-524):
- Validates the device is in LOGIC mode
- Looks up the `srd_decoder*` from `_decoderInfoList`
- Calls `_session->add_decoder(decoder, silent=false, dstatus, sub_decoders, trace)`

**Step 2: SigSession::add_decoder()**

At `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\sigsession.cpp` (lines 1463-1542):
- Creates a `DecoderStack` wrapping the `srd_decoder`
- Creates a `DecodeTrace` for the UI
- Adds any sub-decoders
- Calls `trace->create_popup(true)` (since `silent=false`)

**Step 3: DecodeTrace::create_popup()**

At `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\view\decodetrace.cpp` (lines 764-812):
- Creates a `DecoderOptionsDlg`
- Calls `dlg.load_options(this)` which builds the form
- Shows the dialog with `dlg.exec()`
- On accept, calls `dlg.apply_setting()` which commits probes and options
- Loops if required probes are missing

**Step 4: DecoderOptionsDlg::load_options() -> load_options_view() -> load_decoder_forms() -> create_decoder_form()**

At `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\dialogs\decoderoptionsdlg.cpp` (lines 389-484):
- For each decoder in the stack, creates a `QFormLayout`
- Adds mandatory channel probes (`decoder->channels`) as `DsComboBox` selectors
- Adds optional channel probes (`decoder->opt_channels`) as `DsComboBox` selectors
- Creates `DecoderOptions` binding (line 470): `auto binding = new prop::binding::DecoderOptions(_trace->decoder(), dec);`
- Calls `binding->add_properties_to_form(decoder_form, false, font)` -- `auto_commit=false` means options are only committed when the user clicks OK
- Wraps everything in a `DecoderGroupBox`

**Step 5: User clicks OK -> apply_setting()**

At `c:\Users\admin\Downloads\DSView-main_2026_4_27cppnb\PXView\pv\dialogs\decoderoptionsdlg.cpp` (lines 546-552):
```cpp
void DecoderOptionsDlg::apply_setting()
{
    commit_probes();
    for (auto b : _bindings) {
        b->commit();
    }
}
```

This calls `Binding::commit()` which iterates all properties and calls each property's `commit()`, which reads the widget value and calls the setter, which calls `Decoder::set_option()`, which stores the GVariant in `Decoder::_options`.
